		<div class="et_pb_section et_pb_section_0 et_pb_with_background et_section_regular" >
				
				
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_row et_pb_row_0 et_pb_gutters1">

				<div class="et_pb_text_inner"><h2 id="facts-figures">Facts &amp; Figures</h2></div>
			</div>
			</div>
				
				
				
				
			</div>
				
				
			</div><div class="et_pb_section et_pb_section_1 et_pb_with_background et_section_regular" >
				
				
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_row et_pb_row_1">
				<div class="et_pb_column et_pb_column_1_2 et_pb_column_1  et_pb_css_mix_blend_mode_passthrough">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_1  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_light">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_text_inner"><h2 id="albuquerque-international-sunport">albuquerque international sunport</h2></div>
			</div><div class="et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_2 et_animated  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_light">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_text_inner"><p>The Albuquerque International Sunport, known for its distinct southwestern architecture and cultural décor, is New Mexico&#8217;s largest commercial airport, welcoming over 5 million passengers each year. The Sunport boasts a large art collection with rotating special exhibits, New Mexican cuisine, and many local artisanal gifts. Served by 8 major carriers, the Sunport offers non-stop service between ABQ and 32 destinations, with worldwide connectivity. The Albuquerque International Sunport is the Gateway of New Mexico.</p>
<p>The Sunport is more than just an airport – it is a cultural gateway that welcomes tourists from all over the world. It is home to distinctive architecture, outstanding <a href="https://www.abqsunport.com/sunport-arts-program/">collections of southwestern art</a>, <a href="https://www.abqsunport.com/shops-restaurants/">delicious local cuisine</a>, and <a href="https://www.abqsunport.com/shops-restaurants/">distinctive gift shops</a>, with <a href="https://www.abqsunport.com/sunport-amenities/">many amenities</a> to offer passengers and visitors.</p>
<p>The Sunport is owned and operated by the <a href="https://www.cabq.gov" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">City of Albuquerque</a> and is committed to providing a safe, clean, and passenger-friendly facility for business and leisure travelers alike.</p></div>
			</div>
			</div><div class="et_pb_column et_pb_column_1_2 et_pb_column_2  et_pb_css_mix_blend_mode_passthrough et-last-child">

				<div class="et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_3  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_light">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_text_inner"><h2 id="sunport-fast-facts">sunport fast facts</h2></div>
			</div><div class="et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_4 et_animated  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_light">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_text_inner"><ul>
<li>ABQ welcomed <strong>5,274,764</strong> passengers in 2025</li>
<li><span style="font-size: 17px;">There were <strong>155,854</strong> takeoffs and landings, for an average of <strong>427</strong> <strong>per day</strong>, in 2025 including general aviation, and military.</span></li>
<li><span style="font-size: 17px;">ABQ moved <strong>83,355,710</strong> pounds<span> of cargo in 2025.</span></span></li>
<li>ABQ is served by 6 major airlines: <a href="http://www.alaskaair.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Alaska Airlines</a>, <a href="http://www.aa.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">American</a>, <a href="http://www.delta.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Delta</a>, <a href="http://www.jetblue.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">jetBlue</a>, <a href="http://www.southwest.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Southwest</a>, and <a href="http://www.united.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">United</a>. It also receives regular service from commuter airlines <a href="https://www.advancedairlines.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Advanced</a> and <a href="https://contourairlines.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Contour.</a></li>
<li>ABQ handles air cargo from Amazon Air, <a href="https://www.fedex.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">FedEx,</a> and <a href="https://www.ups.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">UPS.</a></li>
<li>ABQ offers <a href="https://www.abqsunport.com/wherewefly/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">nonstop service</a> to more than 30 destinations.</li>
</ul></div>
			</div>
			</div>
				
				
				
				
			</div>
				
				
			</div><div class="et_pb_section et_pb_section_4 et_pb_section_parallax et_pb_with_background et_section_regular" >
				
				<span class="et_parallax_bg_wrap"><span
						class="et_parallax_bg et_pb_parallax_css"
						style="background-image: url(https://www.abqsunport.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/12/Abq-Sunport_CABQ_High-Res-10-scaled.jpg);"
					></span></span>
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_row et_pb_row_5">
				<div class="et_pb_column et_pb_column_4_4 et_pb_column_9  et_pb_css_mix_blend_mode_passthrough et-last-child">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_5  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_light">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_text_inner"><h1 style="text-align: center;"><span style="color: #b94700; font-family: _Insignia; font-weight: normal;">sunport mission</span></h1>
<h2 style="text-align: center;" id="our-mission-is-to-provide-a-safe-authentic-and-exceptional-new-mexico-experiencethat-connects-families-businesses-and-cultures"><span style="font-size: x-large;"><span style="color: #00778b; font-family: _Insignia; font-weight: normal;">our mission is to provide a safe, authentic, and exceptional new mexico experience<br />that connects families, businesses and cultures<br /></span></span></h2>
<p><span style="color: #b94700; font-family: _Insignia; font-weight: normal;"></span></p>
<p><span style="color: #b94700; font-family: _Insignia; font-weight: normal;"></span></p></div>
			</div>
			</div>
				
				
				
				
			</div>
				
				
			</div><div class="et_pb_section et_pb_section_5 et_pb_with_background et_section_regular" >
				
				
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_row et_pb_row_6">
				<div class="et_pb_column et_pb_column_1_2 et_pb_column_10  et_pb_css_mix_blend_mode_passthrough">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_6  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_light">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_text_inner"><h2 id="sunport-facilities">sunport facilities</h2></div>
			</div><div class="et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_7 et_animated  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_light">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_text_inner"><ul>
<li>The Terminal Building has approximately 600,000 sq. ft. of total space, including 22 gates in the A and B Concourses used by the major commercial air carriers and the C Gate area which accommodates commuter airlines.</li>
<li>ABQ’s elevation is 5,355 feet. Its latitude and longitude are 35 degrees, 02 minutes North and 106 degrees, 37 minutes West.</li>
</ul></div>
			</div><div class="et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_8  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_light">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_text_inner"><h2 id="airfield">airfield</h2></div>
			</div><div class="et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_9 et_animated  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_light">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_text_inner"><ul>
<li>RWY 08/26: east-west air carrier runway, 13,793 ft. </li>
<li>RWY 03/21: northeast-southwest air carrier runway, 10,000 ft.</li>
<li>RWY 12/30: northwest-southeast general aviation runway, 6,000 ft.</li>
<li>Two fixed-base operators: <a href="http://cutteraviation.com/airport-facility-locations/abq-albuquerque-nm/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Cutter Aviation</a> and <a href="https://www.atlanticaviation.com/location/ABQ" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Atlantic Aviation</a></li>
</ul></div>
			</div>
			</div><div class="et_pb_column et_pb_column_1_2 et_pb_column_11  et_pb_css_mix_blend_mode_passthrough et-last-child">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_module et_pb_image et_pb_image_1">
				
				
				
				
				<a href="https://www.abqsunport.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/08/ABQ-Sunport-Runways_Diagram.jpg" class="et_pb_lightbox_image" title=""><span class="et_pb_image_wrap "><img decoding="async" width="1020" height="624" src="https://www.abqsunport.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/08/ABQ-Sunport-Runways_Diagram.jpg" alt="" title="ABQ-Sunport-Runways_Diagram" srcset="https://www.abqsunport.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/08/ABQ-Sunport-Runways_Diagram.jpg 1020w, https://www.abqsunport.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/08/ABQ-Sunport-Runways_Diagram-980x600.jpg 980w, https://www.abqsunport.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/08/ABQ-Sunport-Runways_Diagram-480x294.jpg 480w" sizes="(min-width: 0px) and (max-width: 480px) 480px, (min-width: 481px) and (max-width: 980px) 980px, (min-width: 981px) 1020px, 100vw" class="wp-image-1361" /></span></a>
			</div>
			</div>
				
				
				
				
			</div><div class="et_pb_row et_pb_row_7">
				<div class="et_pb_column et_pb_column_4_4 et_pb_column_12  et_pb_css_mix_blend_mode_passthrough et-last-child">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_10  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_light">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_text_inner"><h2 id="federal-facilities">federal facilities</h2></div>
			</div><div class="et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_11 et_animated  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_light">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_text_inner"><ul>
<li>ABQ shares its runways with <a href="https://www.kirtland.af.mil" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Kirtland Air Force Base</a>, which provides aircraft rescue and firefighting (<a href="https://www.faa.gov/airports/airport_safety/aircraft_rescue_fire_fighting/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">ARFF</a>) services for the airport.</li>
<li>Federal Aviation Administration (<a href="https://www.faa.gov" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">FAA</a>) facilities in Albuquerque include the<a href="https://www.faa.gov/about/office_org/headquarters_offices/ato/service_units/air_traffic_services/atct/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"> Air Traffic Control Tower</a> and the <a href="https://www.faa.gov/about/office_org/field_offices/fsdo/?state=NM" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Flight Standards District Office</a>.</li>
<li>The Transportation Security Administration (<a href="https://www.tsa.gov" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">TSA</a>) occupies office space at the historic Old Terminal Building and at the security-screening checkpoint.</li>
<li>Customs and Border Protection (<a href="https://www.cbp.gov" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">CBP</a>), which is responsible for processing international flights and international cargo, is ABQ’s point of entry.</li>
<li><a href="https://www.weather.gov" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">The National Weather Service</a> and the <a href="https://www.usps.com" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">U.S. Postal Service Express Mail Facility</a> are also located at the airport.</li>
</ul></div>
			</div>
			</div>
				
				
				
				
			</div><div class="et_pb_row et_pb_row_8">
				<div class="et_pb_column et_pb_column_4_4 et_pb_column_13  et_pb_css_mix_blend_mode_passthrough et-last-child">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_12  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_light">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_text_inner"><h2 id="economic-impact">economic impact</h2></div>
			</div><div class="et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_13 et_animated  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_light">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_text_inner"><ul>
<li>Over 3,000 are people are employed at ABQ.</li>
<li>An economic impact study conducted in 2019 by the University of New Mexico concluded that the Albuquerque International Sunport is responsible for $2.225 billion in economic output for the state and 19,000 jobs are directly or indirectly related to the facility.</li>
<li>If you’re interested in development opportunities with the Aviation Department, visit our <a href="https://abqsunportbiz.com" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Doing Business</a> website.</li>
</ul></div>
			</div>
			</div>
				
				
				
				
			</div><div class="et_pb_row et_pb_row_9">
				<div class="et_pb_column et_pb_column_4_4 et_pb_column_14  et_pb_css_mix_blend_mode_passthrough et-last-child">

			</div><div class="et_pb_section et_pb_section_6 et_pb_with_background et_section_regular" >
				
				
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_row et_pb_row_11">
				<div class="et_pb_column et_pb_column_4_4 et_pb_column_16  et_pb_css_mix_blend_mode_passthrough et-last-child">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_18  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_light">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_text_inner"><h2 id="airport-master-plan">airport master plan</h2></div>
			</div><div class="et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_19 et_animated  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_light">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_text_inner"><p>The Albuquerque International Sunport&#8217;s Sustainable Airport Master Plan was initiated by the <a href="https://www.cabq.gov" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">City of Albuquerque</a>. An airport master plan is intended to evaluate the Airport’s capabilities and roles to forecast future aviation demand, and to plan for the timely development of new or improved facilities that may be required to meet that demand. The ultimate goal of the master plan is to provide systematic guidelines for the airport’s overall maintenance, development, and operation. This planning effort is unique, as it will include sustainable planning goals and objectives.</p>
<p>To learn more about the Sustainable Airport Master Plan, <a href="https://www.abqsunport.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/12/ABQ_Executive_Summary-ReducedSize.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" title="Master Plan Executive Summary">view the Executive Summary here</a>.</p></div>
			</div>
			</div>
				
				
				
				
			</div><div class="et_pb_row et_pb_row_12">
				<div class="et_pb_column et_pb_column_4_4 et_pb_column_17  et_pb_css_mix_blend_mode_passthrough et-last-child">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_20  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_light">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_text_inner"><h2 id="tarmac-delay-contingency-plan">tarmac delay contingency plan</h2></div>
			</div><div class="et_pb_module et_pb_text et_pb_text_21 et_animated  et_pb_text_align_left et_pb_bg_layout_light">
				
				
				
				
				<div class="et_pb_text_inner"><p>The Albuquerque International Sunport has developed a <a href="https://www.abqsunport.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/ABQ-DOT-Tarmac-Delay-Contingency-Plan-ABQ-07-11-2023.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" title="Tarmac Delay Contingency Plan">Tarmac Delay Contingency Plan </a>pursuant to 49 U.S.C § 42301(c) (Section 415 of the FAA Modernization and Reform Act of 2012, P.L. 112-95 (2012)).</p></div>
			</div>

<script>


    //Render SeedProd Notification Bar Pro
    jQuery(window).bind("load", function() {
    jQuery(document).ready(function($){
        
                    var schedule_start_date = '1791088526';
            var schedule_end_date = '1791088526';
                
        var current_timestamp = Math.floor(new Date().getTime() / 1000);
        var status = 1;
        var show = true;
        if(status == 2){

        if(current_timestamp > schedule_start_date  && current_timestamp < schedule_end_date){
            var show = true;
        }else{
            var show = false;
        }

        }
        var endDate = new Date();
        var now = new Date();
        var is_preview = false;
        if(is_preview){
            var show = true;
        }
        
        

        if(show){
                    jQuery('body').prepend(jQuery('#wpnbio-show'));
            jQuery('body').prepend(jQuery('#wpnb-bar'));
                
        var wpnb_bar_state = wpnbCookies.get('wpnb_3');
        if(wpnb_bar_state == undefined ){
                            show_wpnb_bar();
                    }else if(wpnb_bar_state == 'shown'){
            show_wpnb_bar();
        }
        if(wpnb_bar_state == 'hidden' ){
            $( "#wpnbio-show" ).show();
            //console.log(wpnb_bar_state);
        }
        

        // show hide logic
        $( "#wpnbio-close" ).click(function() {
            hide_wpnb_bar();
        });

        $( "#wpnbio-show" ).click(function() {
            $(this).hide();
            if(jQuery("#wpnb-bar").css('position') == 'relative'){
                $('html,body').scrollTop(0);
            }
            show_wpnb_bar();
        });

                }


    });
    });

    function hide_wpnb_bar(){
            // jQuery('#wpnb-bar').one('webkitAnimationEnd mozAnimationEnd MSAnimationEnd oanimationend animationend',function(){ jQuery("#wpnb-bar").hide();});
            // jQuery("#wpnb-bar").addClass('animated fadeOutUpBig');
            jQuery("#wpnb-bar").hide();
            jQuery("#wpnbio-show").fadeIn();

            jQuery('html').attr('style','margin-top:'+ 0 +'px');
            
            wpnbCookies.set('wpnb_3', 'hidden');
    }

    function show_wpnb_bar(){



        // Calcuate body top margin if fixed.
                if(jQuery("#wpnb-bar").css('position') == 'fixed'){       
            // Calcute Position if WP Admin bar is active
                        if(jQuery('#wpadminbar').length >= 1){
                // Logged in
                var adminBarHeight = jQuery("#wpadminbar").height();
                jQuery('#wpnb-bar').css('top',jQuery("#wpadminbar").height()+'px');
                var htmlHeight = jQuery("#wpnb-bar").height() +10 + 0 + adminBarHeight;
                jQuery('html').attr('style','margin-top:'+ htmlHeight +'px !important');

            }else{
                // Logged out
                setTimeout(function(){ 
                         var marginTop = jQuery("#wpnb-bar").height() +10 + 0;
                        jQuery('html').attr('style','margin-top:'+ marginTop +'px !important');
                }, 100);
            }
                    }
        
        // Animation
                jQuery("#wpnb-bar").show();
        wpnbCookies.set('wpnb_3', 'shown');
            }
</script>
